### [237] [EgoProbe: Benchmarking Long-Range and Exact Memory in Streaming Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17128)
*Haokai Pang,Yuqian Fu,Yifei Wang,Yujiao Shen,Tianwen Qian,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Hongbo Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EgoProbe 基准，从"多久前"（长时程保持）与"多精确"（干扰下的事件特定回忆）两个维度评估流式第一人称视频记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频是 wearable AI 助手的天然基础，流式系统需边到边处理观测，而查询可能指向久远的过去。现有工作只探索了跨长时隙的信息回忆，但"记得多远"只是挑战的一半：重复出现的物体、动作和地点要求模型精确记住哪个细节属于哪个事件。

Method: 构建于长时程第一人称视频之上，采用"事件接地"的数据引擎来延长回忆间隔、识别相关事件并构造基于事件的干扰项，随后进行人工验证。包含两个子集：EgoProbe-Base（无中间相关事件的长程回忆）和 EgoProbe-Echo（含相似中间事件与事件接地干扰项）。Echo 采用粗到细的多轮探测：第一轮评估粗粒度事件回忆，第二轮测试模型能否检索指定事件对应的目标细节。

Result: 对多种模型的广泛评估显示长程回忆存在显著局限，在相关经历中恢复目标事件细节更加困难。即使模型正确回答了粗粒度问题，也常常无法回忆起指定事件的具体细节。

Conclusion: EgoProbe 揭示了这一未被充分探索的失败模式，为推进流式第一人称视频理解中的长程与精确记忆提供了测试平台。

Abstract: Egocentric video provides a continuous first-person record of everyday activities, making it a natural foundation for wearable AI assistants. Unlike offline video understanding, streaming systems must process observations as they arrive, while queries may refer to experiences from the distant past. Recent work has begun to explore whether information can be recalled across long temporal gaps. Yet remembering how far is only part of the challenge: recurring objects, actions, and locations also require models to remember exactly which detail belongs to which event. We introduce EgoProbe, a benchmark that evaluates streaming egocentric memory along these two complementary dimensions: long-range retention, i.e., how far, and event-specific recall under interference, i.e., how exact. Built on long-form egocentric videos, EgoProbe uses an event-grounded data engine to extend recall intervals, identify related events, and construct event-grounded distractors, followed by human verification. It contains two subsets: EgoProbe-Base, which evaluates long-range recall without intervening related events, and EgoProbe-Echo, which challenges models with similar intervening events and event-grounded distractors. EgoProbe-Echo adopts coarse-to-fine multi-turn probing: the first turn evaluates coarse-grained event recall, while the second tests whether the model can retrieve the target-specific detail associated with the designated event. Extensive evaluation across diverse models reveals substantial limitations in long-range recall, with further challenges in recovering target-event details among related experiences. Even when models correctly answer coarse-grained questions, they often fail to recall the specific details of the designated event. EgoProbe exposes this underexplored failure mode and provides a testbed for advancing long-range and exact memory in streaming egocentric video understanding.

</details>


### [238] [MARS: Systematic Multi-Level Acceleration for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17141)
*Zengyuan Zuo,Yongchang Zhang,Guangyu Zhao,Tianze Zhang,Biao Wu,Meng Fang,Yiwu Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MARS 是一个免训练、即插即用的多层级世界动作模型（WAM）加速框架，通过选择性地消除冗余计算，在保持任务成功率的同时实现最高 4.00 倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）在具身智能中通过联合预测未来世界状态与机器人动作展现出潜力，但其沉重的视觉编码和迭代式生成推理带来显著延迟，限制了其在高频闭环控制中的应用。现有免训练加速方法通常只优化单一阶段，在激进复用下速度提升有限或导致控制性能退化。

Method: 作者系统性地剖析了 WAM 的推理开销，发现 Transformer 计算、扩散去噪和连续策略查询中存在大量冗余，但冗余的安全移除取决于其对动作预测与控制敏感度的影响。基于此提出 MARS，一个免训练、即插即用的多层级加速框架，包含三项互补机制：选择性移除低影响的注意力计算、依据运动粒度自适应加速扩散推理、以及在连续查询间推测性复用预测的未来视觉表征并进行在线验证。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-PRO 和 RoboCasa 上的大量实验表明，MARS 分别实现了最高 4.00×、3.94× 和 2.10× 的端到端加速，同时任务成功率接近原始策略。此外，MARS 可跨不同模型骨干泛化，并适用于仿真与真机实验，显著改善了 WAM 推理的效率-性能权衡。

Conclusion: MARS 通过多层级、按敏感度安全消除冗余的免训练加速策略，有效缓解了 WAM 推理延迟问题，在保持控制性能的同时大幅提升效率，具有跨骨干、跨仿真与真机的良好泛化能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising alternative for embodied intelligence by jointly predicting future world states and robot actions. However, their heavy visual encoding and iterative generative inference incur substantial latency, limiting their applicability to high-frequency closed-loop control. Existing training-free acceleration methods typically optimize a single stage, yielding limited speedups or degraded control under aggressive reuse. In this work, we systematically profile the inference overhead of WAMs and observe abundant redundancy across Transformer computation, diffusion denoising, and consecutive policy queries. However, the safe removal of such redundancy depends strongly on its impact on action prediction and control sensitivity. Based on these observations, we introduce \textbf{MARS}, a training-free and plug-and-play framework for multi-level WAM acceleration. MARS selectively removes low-impact attention computation, adaptively accelerates diffusion inference according to motion granularity, and speculatively reuses predicted future visual representations across consecutive queries with online verification. Together, these complementary mechanisms provide remarkable acceleration while limiting approximation error. Extensive experiments across LIBERO, LIBERO-PRO, and RoboCasa show that MARS achieves up to $4.00\times$, $3.94\times$, and $2.10\times$ end-to-end speedups, respectively, while maintaining task success rate close to the original policy. Moreover, MARS generalizes across different model backbones and across simulation and real-robot experiments, substantially improving the efficiency--performance trade-off of WAM inference. Our code is available at https://github.com/PKU-VaLuE-Lab/MARS.

</details>


### [239] [Multics: Generative Multi-Player World Model with Hierarchical Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17201)
*Kaiyi Huang,Wangbo Yu,Yukun Huang,Jiwen Yu,Kunhao Liu,Donglin Yang,Wenbo Hu,Ying Shan,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Multics：面向多玩家交互世界模型的分层通信视频生成框架，通过玩家-组-全局三级信息交换解决全连接通信的二次开销与集中式表示的干扰问题，并在多玩家数量下实现可扩展、状态最优的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多玩家交互世界模型需要模拟多视角共享环境，但现有方法存在两大痛点：全连接通信（如 Solaris）随玩家数增长带来二次注意力开销；集中式方法（如 γ-World）在共享全局表示中造成无关事件之间的干扰。因此需要一种既能扩展到更多玩家、又能抑制不相关事件干扰的通信机制。

Method: 提出 Multics 分层通信框架，在玩家、组、全局三个层级组织信息交换。引入组 token 作为中间层表示介入自注意力，聚合局部相关玩家信息；组 token 与全局 token 相连实现跨组通信，同时分层拓扑限制无关局部事件的直接混合。组 token 参数在各组槽位间共享，使同一通信模块可适配不同玩家数量而无需新增组专用参数。此外提出基于近期相机轨迹的预算约束动态分组，自适应聚合相关玩家并限制多余通信。

Result: 在 4 玩家和 6 玩家设置下达到状态最优性能，跨玩家一致性、玩家特定动作与相机可控性、抗干扰鲁棒性均提升。可从 4 玩家训练泛化到 6、8、10 玩家推理而无需重训练，同时相比全连接通信降低了自注意力计算量。

Conclusion: Multics 通过分层通信与动态分组，在可扩展性、生成质量与计算效率之间取得良好平衡，为多玩家交互世界模型提供了一种可泛化、可扩展的通信范式。

Abstract: Multi-player interactive world models aim to simulate shared environments from multiple players' perspectives, but achieving robust generation while scaling to more players remains challenging. Existing approaches that rely on fully-connected communication (e.g., Solaris) incur quadratic attention costs as the number of players grows, while centralized approaches (e.g., $γ$-World) suffer from interference between unrelated events in a shared global representation. To address these challenges, we present Multics, a multi-player video generation framework with hierarchical communication that structures information exchange across player, group, and global levels. This hierarchy introduces group tokens into self-attention as intermediate group-level representations that aggregate locally relevant player information. These tokens connect to global tokens for cross-group communication, while the hierarchical topology limits direct mixing between unrelated local events. Group-token parameters are shared across group slots, allowing the same communication module to accommodate varying numbers of players without introducing new group-specific parameters. We further propose budget-constrained dynamic grouping based on recent camera trajectories, which adaptively groups relevant players while limiting unnecessary communication. Experiments demonstrate that Multics achieves state-of-the-art performance in 4- and 6-player settings, with improved cross-player consistency, player-specific action and camera controllability, and interference robustness. Moreover, Multics generalizes from 4-player training to 6-, 8-, and 10-player inference without retraining, while reducing self-attention computation compared with fully-connected communication.

</details>


### [240] [AReA: Adaptive Receptive-Field Attention for Efficient High-Resolution Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17227)
*Javad Rajabi,Koorosh Roohi,David B. Lindell,Babak Taati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的注意力机制AReA，通过由粗到细、随去噪进程演化的感受野，解决Diffusion Transformers生成超出训练分辨率图像时的结构失真与细节退化问题，同时提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers（DiTs）在生成超出训练分辨率的图像时面临困难：分辨率提升使token数量快速增长，全注意力计算代价呈二次方上升；同时模型在每步都关注所有token，会暴露于超出训练范围的token数量和相对距离，导致分布外问题，输出出现结构失真和细节退化。作者将统一的全局全注意力视为这两类问题的共同根源，并观察到去噪过程中注意力有效感受野会从早期全局（形成结构）逐渐收窄到后期局部（精修细节）。

Method: 提出AReA，一种无需训练的注意力机制，其感受野随去噪进程由粗到细演化。在早期步骤中，对下采样的key和value执行跨尺度粗粒度注意力，以捕捉全局上下文；在后期步骤中，则在逐步缩小的窗口内执行局部注意力。

Result: 无需额外参数或训练，实验表明AReA在多种目标分辨率和不同模型上持续优于当前最先进的无训练基线方法，同时速度更快、效率更高。

Conclusion: 统一的全注意力是超分辨率生成中结构失真与效率低下的共同原因；通过让注意力感受野随去噪由粗到细演化，可在不训练、不增加参数的情况下同时改善生成质量与计算效率。

Abstract: Generating images beyond the training resolution of Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) remains challenging, as the number of tokens grows rapidly with resolution, full attention becomes expensive, and outputs often suffer from structural distortions and degraded detail. We identify uniform full attention as the common source of both problems. Attending to all tokens at every step causes quadratic cost and exposes the model to more tokens and relative distances beyond the training range, pushing the model out of distribution. We observe that attention's effective receptive field narrows during denoising, from global in early steps that form structure to local in later steps that refine details. We propose AReA, a training-free attention mechanism whose receptive field evolves from coarse to fine, following the denoising progression. In early steps, AReA applies cross-scale coarse attention over downsampled keys and values, capturing global context, while in later steps it applies local attention within progressively shrinking windows. Without additional parameters or training, experiments show that AReA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art training-free baselines across target resolutions and different models, while also being faster and more efficient.

</details>


### [241] [Beyond Sparse Captions: Aligning Slide-Level Text and Patch-Level Vision in Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17233)
*Daniel Shao,Dongmin Bang,Luca L. Weishaupt,Sophia J. Wagner,Nic G. Reitsam,Ming Y. Lu,Long P. Le,Richard J. Chen,Faisal Mahmood*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LIMIT预训练方法，利用临床病理报告对文本编码器进行微调，构建CONCH-Z模型，在22项任务上实现零样本SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算病理学中的对比视觉-语言预训练依赖社交媒体、教学视频和论文等策展的图像-文本数据集，但这些语料相对组织形态多样性稀疏，且难以扩展，因为病理学家在临床实践中并不常规生成描述性文本。作者希望利用诊断工作流程中可规模化生成的幻灯片级病理报告。

Method: 提出Locked Image Multiple Instance PreTraining (LIMIT)。核心思路：冻结预训练视觉编码器，仅微调文本编码器。报告嵌入作为跨注意力查询，作用于补丁特征包之上，生成报告条件下的幻灯片表示，并通过InfoNCE优化。基于LIMIT构建CONCH-Z，将CONCH v1.5的文本编码器对齐到33.5万份临床报告。

Result: 在覆盖补丁级分类、肿瘤检测和幻灯片级亚型分类的22项任务上评估，CONCH-Z在零样本性能上超越基于策展文本数据集训练的视觉-语言编码器，达到SOTA，同时缩小了与使用更多参数量和计算资源的幻灯片级基础模型之间的差距。

Conclusion: 利用临床诊断流程中自然产生的幻灯片级病理报告进行弱监督视觉-语言预训练是可行且有效的，LIMIT方法通过冻结视觉编码器、仅微调文本编码器的方式有效弥合了宏观报告描述与微观补丁特征之间的鸿沟。代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language (VL) pretraining in computational pathology has generally relied on curated image-caption datasets sourced from social media, educational videos, and research publications. These corpora are sparse relative to the diversity of tissue morphology and difficult to scale further, as pathologists do not routinely produce descriptive captions in clinical practice. We propose to instead leverage slide-level pathology reports which can be generated during routine diagnostic workflows in a scalable manner. A key challenge in this weakly-supervised setting is that pathology reports describe macroscopic, diagnostic findings while patch-level visual features describe microscopic findings. We introduce Locked Image Multiple Instance PreTraining (LIMIT), which addresses this gap by freezing a pretrained vision encoder and fine-tuning only the text encoder: report embeddings serve as cross-attention queries over bags of patch features, producing report-conditioned slide representations optimized via InfoNCE. Using LIMIT, we establish CONCH-Z, which aligns the text encoder of CONCH v1.5 to 335k clinical reports. Evaluated across 22 tasks spanning patch-level classification, tumor detection, and slide-level subtyping, CONCH-Z establishes state-of-the-art zero-shot performance over VL encoders trained on curated caption datasets, while simultaneously closing the gap with slide-level foundation models trained with substantially more parameters and compute. The code and model is available at https://github.com/mahmoodlab/LIMIT.

</details>


### [242] [When Left Is Right? Camera-Motion Understanding beyond Screen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17242)
*Chong Cheng,Ran Zhang,Xinyuan Li,Alan Zhao,Hao Wang,Xi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CameraVLM，通过将相机运动状态表示与屏幕流动态耦合，并让语言模型参与解释，解决了视觉语言模型难以判断相机左右运动的问题，在多个基准和人类对比中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉语言模型虽能解决视觉谜题和追踪长视频，但在判断相机是向左还是向右移动这类相机运动理解任务上，表现仅相当于随机猜测。已有方法（如加入相机运动数据或几何表示）单独使用均无法弥合这一差距，因此需要新的思路。

Method: 作者提出CameraVLM，包含两个核心条件：一是表示需将屏幕外的相机运动与屏幕内的光流动态耦合，二是必须由语言模型来解读而非训练为独立的姿态或深度估计器。具体方法包括配对运动状态表示与状态-语言对齐：相机token捕捉相机在屏幕外的运动，光流token捕捉屏幕内主体和背景的运动，并采用两阶段训练策略使语言始终在环路中，从而共同学习相机运动语义。

Result: 仅有9B参数的CameraVLM在三个基准和超过20个基线上大幅超越所有开源模型及相机运动专用模型，并在48人参与的研究中匹配或超过GPT-6 Astra以及人类观众的表现。

Conclusion: 相机运动理解需要同时满足表示耦合和语言参与两个条件，CameraVLM验证了这一思路的有效性，以较小参数量取得了领先性能。

Abstract: Can a vision-language model tell left from right? Modern VLMs solve visual puzzles and follow hour-long videos, yet when asked whether the camera moved left or right, most do no better than a coin flip. Adding camera-motion data or geometric representations has been tried, separately, and neither closes the gap. We argue that camera-motion understanding needs two conditions at once: the representation must couple off-screen camera motion with on-screen flow dynamics, and it must be interpreted by the language model rather than trained as a standalone pose or depth estimator. We propose CameraVLM, which pairs a motion-state representation with state-language alignment. Camera tokens capture how the camera moves off screen, flow tokens capture how the subject and background move on screen, and a two-stage training strategy keeps language in the loop so that both are learned together with camera-motion semantics. With only 9B parameters, CameraVLM outperforms all open-source and camera-motion-specific models by a wide margin across three benchmarks and more than 20 baselines, and matches or surpasses GPT-6 Astra as well as human viewers in a 48-participant study. Project page: https://3dagentworld.github.io/CameraVLM/

</details>


### [243] [HybridDualPathNet: Cross-Fusion Attention of EfficientNetV2 and Swin Transformer for Brain MRI Classification of Alzheimer's and Parkinson's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17251)
*Jarpula Nirjala,Rahul Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HybridDualPathNet 混合网络用于 AD/PD/HC 的结构 MRI 分类，强调数据划分方式与跨数据集评估对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD、PD 与健康对照的结构 MRI 疾病特征存在重叠，模型性能易受数据划分方式影响，单数据集高分未必代表可靠泛化。

Method: 融合 EfficientNetV2-S 提取局部特征与 Swin-Tiny 提取全局上下文，并通过 Cross-Fusion Attention（CFA）模块进行跨分支融合；开展两组实验：(1) Kaggle APD-3-Class 数据集图像级划分；(2) Kaggle 与 ADNI 合并共 17,859 张切片，采用按来源划分并对 ADNI 受试者做患者级隔离；同时使用温度缩放、分裂保形预测及 Grad-CAM++、Integrated Gradients、遮挡等显著图分析。

Result: Kaggle 图像级划分准确率 98.25%；合并数据集患者级划分下标准准确率 73.37%、宏平均 AUC 0.880；按来源分层评估显示域差距显著（Kaggle 91.2%，ADNI 59.2%）；温度缩放将期望校准误差从 0.130 降至 0.015，分裂保形预测覆盖率达 89.5%；显著图显示模型关注脑组织区域。

Conclusion: 评估 MRI 分类系统时需重视患者级隔离、跨数据集评估与不确定性估计；单数据集上的优异表现并不必然带来跨影像队列的可靠泛化。

Abstract: Separating Alzheimer's Disease (AD), Parkinson's Disease (PD), and Healthy Controls (HC) using structural MRI remains challenging because disease signatures overlap and model performance can be strongly affected by data partitioning. We present HybridDualPathNet, a hybrid architecture combining EfficientNetV2-S for local features and Swin-Tiny for global context through a Cross-Fusion Attention (CFA) module. We evaluate the model in two experiments. First, on the Kaggle APD-3-Class dataset using an image-level split, the model achieves 98.25% accuracy. Second, on a combined Kaggle and Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) dataset containing 17,859 slices, we use a per-source split with patient-wise separation for ADNI subjects. In this setting, the model achieves 73.37% standard accuracy and a macro AUC of 0.880. Source-stratified evaluation reveals a substantial domain gap, with 91.2% accuracy on Kaggle test images and 59.2% on ADNI test images. Temperature scaling reduces expected calibration error from 0.130 to 0.015, while split-conformal prediction achieves 89.5% coverage. Saliency analyses using Grad-CAM++, Integrated Gradients, and occlusion indicate that model activations concentrate on brain tissue. These results highlight the importance of patient-level separation, cross-dataset evaluation, and uncertainty estimation when assessing MRI classification systems. The findings also show that strong performance on a single dataset does not necessarily translate to reliable generalization across imaging cohorts.

</details>


### [244] [SSM-Attack: Learning Where to Perturb for Black-Box Face Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17323)
*Tunahan Parlayıcı,Erchan Aptoula,Yücel Saygın*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SSM-Attack，一个基于选择性状态空间模型的黑盒人脸隐私保护框架。它不直接合成扰动，而是用状态空间模型一次线性时间预测扰动预算的空间分配，标记携带身份证据的面部位置，再由投影细化确定数值。其三套评测集上的黑盒攻击成功率均达最高（CelebA-HQ 87.10%、LADN 86.90%、FFHQ 81.15%），分配网络仅5.73M参数，且在0.10逐像素上限下显著提升SSIM、PSNR与FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别系统广泛部署，在线分享的照片可能在未经本人同意的情况下被匹配。现有基于生成器的保护方法依赖庞大的预训练骨干、多阶段推理或可见的语义编辑，代价高且不自然。作者希望验证：强人脸隐私保护是否可以不依赖大型生成先验，而只需知道"把少量预算花在何处"。

Method: 提出SSM-Attack黑盒人脸隐私框架，核心是选择性状态空间模型（Selective State Space Model）。它不合成扰动本身，而是在一次线性时间前向中预测扰动预算的空间分配，标出哪些面部位置携带身份证据；随后用一个短的投影细化步骤，在该逐像素预算内确定具体数值。整个分配网络仅5.73M参数（含冻结网络共16.13M）。

Result: 在三套评测集上取得最高平均黑盒攻击成功率：CelebA-HQ 87.10%、LADN 86.90%、FFHQ 81.15%（后者来自从未见过该数据集的网络）。在最大逐像素变化上限0.10下，与均匀分配图相比，该分配策略将结构相似度提升0.044、峰值信噪比提升1.62 dB、FID改善11.2点，且仅耗费37%的容量。

Conclusion: 强人脸隐私保护并不需要大型生成先验；只需要知道如何把"小先验"用在正确的地方。基于状态空间模型的预算空间分配为黑盒人脸隐私保护提供了一条轻量、高效且高质量的路径。

Abstract: Face recognition systems are deployed widely enough that a photograph shared online can be matched without its subject's consent. Generator-based protection methods rely on heavy pretrained backbones, multi-stage inference or visible semantic edits. We introduce SSM-Attack, a black-box face privacy framework built on Selective State Space Models. Rather than synthesizing the perturbation, the state-space model predicts in one linear-time pass a spatial allocation of the perturbation budget, marking which facial locations carry identity evidence; a short projected refinement then sets the values within this per-pixel budget. Against published benchmark results, SSM-Attack attains the highest average black-box attack success rate on all three evaluation sets: 87.10 percent on CelebA-HQ, 86.90 on LADN and 81.15 on FFHQ, the last from a network that never saw that dataset. The allocation network holds 5.73 million parameters, or 16.13 million with the frozen networks used by its quality terms. Under the same cap of 0.10 on the maximum per-pixel change, allocation improves structural similarity by 0.044, peak signal-to-noise ratio by 1.62 decibels and Frechet Inception Distance by 11.2 points over a uniform map while spending 37 percent of its capacity. Strong facial privacy protection therefore does not require a large generative prior; it requires knowing where to spend a small one.

</details>


### [245] [HairFlow: Modeling 3D Hair Strands with Multi-Modal Conditioned Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17348)
*Yating Wang,Kui Wu,Ran Yi,Zixuan Lu,Tongtong Wang,Zhongtian Zheng,Lizhuang Ma,Yuefan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HairFlow 提出了一种多模态条件扩散框架，用于高效可控的 3D 头发丝建模，通过粗网格和多视角草图两种互补条件生成发丝，避免了昂贵的逐实例优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 头发丝建模面临复杂发型几何、高质量训练数据稀缺以及输入观测不完整或含噪等挑战。基础模型虽提供更广泛的发型先验，但其输出无法直接用于基于发丝的渲染与仿真，现有方法需要昂贵的逐实例优化，且依赖在有限头发数据上训练的专用预测器，限制了效率与泛化能力。

Method: HairFlow 以两种互补表示为条件：约束全局形状的粗网格，以及指定局部发丝流向的多视角草图。它将 Orientation VAE 与条件扩散 Transformer 结合，生成体场并追踪为显式发丝。为克服配对草图-发丝训练数据缺失，作者引入自动合成管线，生成几何对齐且风格多样的多视角草图。

Result: 实验表明，HairFlow 能在多种发型下生成合理且忠实遵循输入条件的 3D 发丝，显著减少推理时间，并支持灵活的多模态控制。

Conclusion: HairFlow 通过多模态条件扩散实现了高效、可控且泛化性更强的 3D 头发丝建模，为基于发丝的渲染与仿真提供了可用方案。

Abstract: Modeling 3D hair strands is challenging due to complex hairstyle geometry, scarce high-quality training data, and incomplete or noisy input observations. Foundation models offer broader hairstyle priors, but their outputs are not directly usable for strand-based rendering and simulation. Existing approaches require costly per-instance optimization to fit hair priors to meshes from foundation models and 2D orientations from specialized predictors trained on limited hair data, restricting efficiency and generalization. We present HairFlow, a multimodal conditioned diffusion framework for efficient and controllable hair strands modeling. HairFlow conditions strand generation on two complementary representations: a coarse mesh that constrains global shape and multi-view sketches that specify local strand flow. HairFlow combines an Orientation VAE with a conditioned diffusion transformer to generate volumetric fields that are traced into explicit strands. To overcome the lack of paired sketch-strand training data, we introduce an automatic synthesis pipeline that generates geometrically aligned multi-view sketches with diverse styles representative of inference-time inputs. Experiments show that HairFlow generates plausible 3D strands that faithfully follow the input conditions across diverse hairstyles, substantially reduces inference time, and supports flexible multimodal control.

</details>


### [246] [MID: Mask-Image Distributional Divergence for Evaluating Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17354)
*Vincenzo Marcianò,Xiaoming Zhang,Gianluca Guglielmo,Sebastien Ourselin,Michela Antonelli,Maria A. Zuluaga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MID（Mask-Image Distributional Divergence），一种面向医学图像分割的分布级评估指标，通过比较预测集与参考集的图像-掩码联合分布，无需逐样本配对即可评估分割质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像分割评估协议（如 Dice、Hausdorff）依赖逐样本配对的真实标注，导致两大局限：一是无法用于无标注数据的预测评估；二是即便有标注，也难以判断一组预测是否匹配真实标注总体的分布结构。

Method: MID 基于"分割质量是图像-掩码联合分布属性"这一观察——掩码单独看可能解剖合理，但与其对应图像不匹配即为错误。方法上，MID 在不要求样本级对应的情况下，比较参考集与预测集的图像-掩码联合分布，从而同时刻画掩码真实性与图像-掩码一致性。

Result: 在 13 个公开数据集（涵盖 CT、MRI、MRA）上评估：在 56 个器官组的解剖真实感扰动下，MID 无需逐样本真值配对即可跟踪平均 Dice 与 Hausdorff 距离；还能检测跨模态与解剖结构的分布漂移，通过受控打乱实验识别图像-掩码错位，并在真实分割模型上给出可靠排序。

Conclusion: MID 是一种有效的分布级医学图像分割评估指标，支持无配对、无标注场景下的质量评估与分布漂移检测，代码已开源。

Abstract: Current evaluation protocols for medical image segmentation remain largely anchored in sample-wise comparisons, e.g., Dice and Hausdorff distance, which require each prediction to be paired with a ground-truth mask. Yet two limitations arise in practice: they are inapplicable when predictions are made on unlabeled data; and even when annotations are available, they cannot assess whether a collection of predictions matches the distributional structure of the ground-truth population. We introduce MID (Mask-Image Distributional Divergence), a distribution-level evaluation metric for medical image segmentation. MID builds upon the observation that segmentation quality is a property of the joint image-mask distribution: a mask may be anatomically plausible in isolation yet incorrect for the image it accompanies. MID compares the joint distribution of images and masks between a reference set and a set of predictions, without requiring sample-wise correspondence. This captures both mask realism and image-mask consistency. We evaluate MID across 13 publicly available datasets spanning CT, MRI, and MRA. Under anatomically realistic corruptions across 56 organ groups, MID tracks mean Dice and Hausdorff distance without per-sample ground-truth pairing. MID also detects distributional shifts across modalities and anatomies, identifies image-mask misalignment through controlled shuffling experiments, and produces reliable model rankings across real segmentation models. Official implementation available at github.com/erc-caravel/pytorch-mid

</details>


### [247] [WIRE: Weight Space Implicit Representation Encoding of Frozen Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17355)
*Ameen Ali,Tamim Zoabi,Lior Wolf,Chaim Baskin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 WIRE，将视频语言模型中部分视频条件计算从输入激活转移到冻结权重的临时低秩更新，从而同时降低长视频的预填充、内存与生成开销，并在精度上取得优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型将视频表示为解码器输入中的长序列视觉嵌入，每个视觉嵌入都要经过所有解码器层、存入 KV cache 并被每个生成 token 关注，因此长视频会同时提高预填充、内存和生成成本。

Method: WIRE 保留短显式视觉上下文，使更新作为修正而非替代视频。它先用问题条件下的双向选择性扫描将每帧全分辨率嵌入压缩为少量 carrier token，并物理缩短解码器输入；随后在选定解码器层用超网络读取压缩前向过程与被移除的全分辨率特征读出，生成注意力与前馈投影的 LoRA 更新；预填充后合并更新，自回归解码运行未修改的冻结骨干。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL-8B 上，每帧 16 个 carrier token 使视觉流压缩 3.5 倍，256 帧预填充时间降低 3.35 倍，1024 帧降低 4.14 倍，KV cache 同比例缩小，且每 token 解码延迟基本不变；在 256 帧预算下的九个基准设置上取得所评估高效视频语言模型中最高的平均精度。

Conclusion: WIRE 通过权重空间隐式表示编码，在保持甚至提升精度的情况下显著降低长视频处理与生成的重复成本。

Abstract: Video language models represent a video as a long sequence of visual embeddings in a decoder's input. Every visual embedding is processed by every decoder layer during prefill, stored in the key value cache, and attended by every generated token, so a long video raises prefill, memory, and generation cost at once. We present WIRE (Weight Space Implicit Representation Encoding), which reduces this recurring cost by moving part of the video conditioned computation of a frozen video language model from its input activations into a temporary low rank update of its weights. WIRE keeps a short explicit visual context, so the update acts as a correction rather than a replacement of the video. WIRE first compresses the full resolution embeddings of each frame into a small set of carrier tokens using a question conditioned bidirectional selective scan, and physically contracts the decoder input so every decoder layer, cache entry, and decoding step operates on the shorter sequence. Hypernetworks at selected decoder layers read the compressed forward pass together with a readout of the full resolution features removed by contraction and generate LoRA updates to the attention and feedforward projections. The updates are merged after prefill, so autoregressive decoding runs the unmodified frozen backbone. On Qwen3-VL-8B, WIRE with 16 carrier tokens per frame contracts the visual stream by $3.5\times$, reduces decoder prefill time by $3.35\times$ at 256 frames and $4.14\times$ at 1024 frames, shrinks the key value cache proportionally, and keeps per token decoding latency approximately constant. Across nine benchmark settings at 256 frames, it obtains the highest average accuracy among efficient video language models we evaluate under a common 256 frame budget.

</details>


### [248] [Brain2Face: A Study on the Relation of EEG Signals and Spatio-temporal Head Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17356)
*Camilo Andrés Martínez Martínez,Olaf Dünkel,Kartik Teotia,Maurice Rekrut,Christian Theobalt,Marc Habermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索仅用脑电信号（EEG）驱动数字头部虚拟人的可能性，贡献了首个配对的64通道EEG与3D高斯头部虚拟人几何/外观标注数据集，并提出EEG到表情的回归基线模型及评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数字头部虚拟人驱动依赖手工艺术设计或基于图像的面部表演捕捉。作者提出一个问题：能否仅从脑信号（EEG）实现人头几何与外观的时空追踪？由于以往工作多集中于活动与情绪识别，该任务基本未被探索，且缺乏公开高质量数据集与基准。

Method: 1) 构建数据集：将64通道EEG与个性化高斯头部虚拟人的逐帧表情潜码同步。2) 提出基线：设计专门的EEG-to-expression模型，以时序EEG信号为输入，回归3D高斯头部虚拟人的表情潜参数。3) 通过新引入的基准协议验证捕捉与算法设计选择。

Result: 建立并验证了首个EEG驱动头部虚拟人表情恢复的数据集与基准，验证了数据采集流程与算法设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解EEG信号与面部表面及外观变化之间的关系开辟了道路，推动了脑信号驱动数字虚拟人这一新方向。

Abstract: Driving digital head avatars is classically done by hand-crafted artist design or by image-based facial performance capture. In this work, we ask whether spatio-temporal tracking of the humans's head geometry and appearance can be performed solely from brain signals, i.e., Electroencephalography (EEG). Since prior work mostly focused on activity and emotion recognition, this task is relatively unexplored without even having publicly available high-quality datasets and benchmarks. Thus, we contribute a dataset featuring paired EEG and human head geometry as well as appearance annotations. We synchronize 64-channel EEG with per-frame expression latent codes of a personalized Gaussian head avatar. Second, we introduce a baseline to the task of expression recovery of a head avatar from EEG signals. To this end, we propose a dedicated EEG-to-expression model that takes a temporal sequence of EEG signals as input and regresses the latent expression parameters of the 3D Gaussian head avatar. Lastly, we validate our capture and algorithmic design choices through a benchmark protocol that we introduce to this task. We believe this work paves the way towards better understanding of the relation between EEG signals and facial surface as well as appearance changes.

</details>


### [249] [Bottlenecked semantic compression by representation reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17412)
*Xiaoya Tang,Shilong Liu,Daniel Smolyak,Xiwen Li,Quynh Nguyen,Tolga Tasdizen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出重建引导的视觉token压缩框架ReConV，用紧凑的Summary Bank总结完整视觉序列，并保留重建残差大的token，实现性能与效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers处理长视觉token序列时计算成本高，现有压缩方法通常独立对token排序并丢弃被认为不重要或冗余的token，可能损失关键信息。

Method: 提出ReConV框架：使用紧凑的Summary Bank（SMB）总结完整视觉序列，保留SMB未能充分捕获信息的token（即重建残差大的token）。在每个压缩层，用轻量位置条件解码器重建当前token，使共享信息压缩进SMB，同时显式保留重建效果差的细节。

Result: 在图像分类和多模态理解任务上取得良好的性能-效率权衡：在ImageNet上以减少48% GMACs超越未压缩基线，并支持可复用的图像级表示用于高效多轮推理。

Conclusion: 重建引导的token压缩能在保持甚至提升性能的同时显著降低计算成本，并可复用于多轮推理场景。

Abstract: Vision Transformers incur high computational costs when processing long visual token sequences. Existing reduction methods typically rank tokens independently and discard those deemed unimportant or redundant. We propose a reconstruction-guided framework for visual token condensation (ReConV), which summarizes the complete visual sequence with a compact Summary Bank (SMB) and retains tokens whose information is not adequately captured by the SMB, as indicated by their reconstruction residuals. At each compression layer, a lightweight position-conditioned decoder reconstructs the current tokens, leaving shared information compressed in the SMB while explicitly preserving poorly reconstructed details. Experiments on image classification and multimodal understanding demonstrate favorable performance-efficiency trade-offs: ReConV surpasses the uncompressed baseline with 48\% fewer GMACs on ImageNet and enables reusable image-level representations for efficient multi-turn inference. Code is available at TokenDrop.

</details>


### [250] [World-T3: Test-Time Training as Persistent Memory for Real-Time Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17434)
*Junyi Wu,Zekun Li,Chenhongyi Yang,Chen Guo,Lingchen Yang,Yongan Zhang,Xinyi Zhang,Hongbing Hu,Bartek Rymkowski,Srinath Sridhar,Yan Yan,Xijie Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: World-T3是一个相机可控的视频世界模型，通过测试时训练记忆将历史观测固化到模型参数中，实现长时序一致的视频生成，并以20.6 FPS实时生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频世界模型隐式依赖生成模型自身保留历史信息，导致超出活跃上下文后出现空间记忆衰减，难以维持长时序生成中已观测世界的一致性。

Method: 提出World-T3：1）采用测试时训练（TTT）记忆，将过去观测整合进专用模型参数；2）使用递归最小二乘更新规则，通过在线位姿索引记忆更新增量吸收新视觉特征；3）记忆以快权重状态按相机位姿读出场景特征，存储和每步计算恒定；4）通过双教师分布匹配将自回归模型蒸馏为少步生成器，实现实时交互。

Result: 在长时序重访基准上，重访PSNR从13.52提升至18.31 dB，绝对旋转误差从3.344降至1.075，两项指标均优于最强基线；在单张NVIDIA B200 GPU上以1280×704分辨率达到20.6 FPS。

Conclusion: World-T3通过将长时序视觉记忆显式外化到模型参数并结合递归最小二乘更新，有效缓解空间记忆衰减，在保持恒定存储与计算开销的同时实现了高质量、实时的长时序交互式视频生成。

Abstract: Recent advances in video world models have enabled plausible interactive environments, yet long-horizon generation places a fundamental demand on visual memory: the previously observed world must remain consistent as the model continues to generate. Existing autoregressive approaches often implicitly rely on the generative model itself to retain such information, leading to spatial memory decay beyond the active context. We introduce World-T3, a camera-controlled video world model with a test-time training memory that consolidates past observations into dedicated model parameters. To robustly update this memory, we propose a recursive least-squares rule that incrementally absorbs novel visual features via online pose-keyed memory updates. Our TTT memory reads out previously observed scene features from the fast-weight state by camera pose, enabling consistent generation with constant storage and per-step computation regardless of rollout length. To facilitate real-time interactive generation with this robust memory, we further distill the autoregressive model into a few-step generator via dual-teacher distribution matching. On a long-horizon revisit benchmark, World-T3 raises revisit PSNR from 13.52 to 18.31 dB and reduces absolute rotation error from 3.344 to 1.075 relative to the strongest baseline on both metrics, while sustaining 20.6 FPS at 1280 x 704 on a single NVIDIA B200 GPU.

</details>


### [251] [Know-Thy-Not: Training-Free Targeted Negation in Dual-Encoder Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17440)
*DoYoung Kim,Changkyu Choi,Adín Ramírez Rivera,YunSeok Choi,Jee-Hyong Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 双编码器视觉语言模型在否定语义上系统性失效，本文证明这是单嵌入框架固有限制（只能"稀释"被否定概念而无法"拒绝"），并提出免训练的 Know-Thy-Not（KTN）方法及新基准 COCO-Confusion。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双编码器视觉语言模型对否定不敏感，例如把"没有狗的一只猫"与"有一只猫和一只狗"判为几乎相同。作者希望从理论上说明该失败并非偶然，而是单嵌入对比学习框架的内在缺陷。

Method: 作者先做理论分析，指出在对比训练下单嵌入方法最多只能忽略被否定概念（dilution），无法对其出现施加惩罚（rejection）。为此提出免训练的 KTN 方法，在图像侧引入一个"拒绝维度"，直接在视觉表示中对被否定概念进行惩罚。同时构建 COCO-Confusion 基准，该基准必须真正实现拒绝才能通过。

Result: 在 9 种骨干网络上，KTN 在 COCO-Confusion 上的 mAP 相比原始 VLM 最高提升 +25.6，相比最强现有方法提升 +9.6。

Conclusion: 否定失败源于单嵌入框架本身；通过引入图像侧拒绝维度，无需训练即可显著提升模型对否定语义的判别能力。

Abstract: Dual-encoder vision-language models systematically fail at negation, scoring ''a cat without a dog'' nearly identically to ''a cat with a dog.'' We show that this failure is inherent to the single-embedding framework. Under contrastive training, single-embedding methods can at best ignore the negated concept (dilution) but never penalize its presence (rejection). We propose Know-Thy-Not (KTN), a training-free method that introduces an image-side rejection dimension to penalize the negated concept directly in the visual representation. We also introduce COCO-Confusion, the first benchmark requiring genuine rejection to succeed. Across 9 backbones, KTN improves COCO-Confusion mAP by up to +25.6 over vanilla VLMs and +9.6 over the strongest existing method.

</details>


### [252] [TableVision: Recovering Verified Structure from Historical Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17448)
*Todd Y. Zhou,James M. Snyder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将表格结构恢复作为核心问题，提出 TableVision 系统，通过动态规划分块、单元格读取与和校验门控，从历史选举登记册中恢复表格结构并验证数据，在退化页面上显著优于现有开源阅读器与 Amazon Textract。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史登记册普遍存在缺行、跨页表格、列名错位、行列和不一致等问题，现有阅读器虽能正确识别数字，却丢失表格结构（干净面板仅 83.7% 数字归入正确单元格，退化页面 Textract 仅 1/1494 正确），因此需要把表格结构恢复当作首要问题。

Method: TableVision：用动态规划按"有多少列的和等于其最后一行"把卷册切分成块；由见证者读取单元格；门控将每列分为三类——精确对账、标记为忠实于算术有误的页面、以及拒绝。并提出和校验理论，说明其无法检测的错误类型，以及在缺陷率 θ 下最优块约为 θ^{-1/2} 行。

Result: 在七个干净选举面板上，最佳开源阅读器仅 83.7% 数字归位、30 列中正确 11 列；在三个退化 1888 年页面上，Textract 仅 1/1494 正确，TableVision 达 99.2%，且通过精确总计检查释放的 51 列全部正确。在 2201 列威斯康星选举数据上，门控接受 2042、标记 118、拒绝 41；一般选举缺陷率 1.1/1000 单元格，失败率随列长按 1-(1-θ)^R 上升；官方记录确认 933/934 可测被接受和与全部 35 个可测被标记总计；部署为 930 万行数据集新增 491,359 条门控选区行。

Conclusion: 验证块的设计至关重要；将表格结构恢复作为核心问题并结合和校验门控，可在大规模历史数据上实现高精度结构恢复与可靠的自动验证。

Abstract: Gaps, skips, and mistakes are the normal state of a historical register: rows go missing, tables cross page breaks, columns slip under wrong names, and column sums disagree with the corresponding row entries. Readers read digits correctly but lose the table structure. On seven clean panels of election returns, the best open-source reader reproduces 99.0\% of the digits but files only 83.7\% of the figures in the correct cells and gets 11 of 30 columns correct. TableVision treats table structure recovery as the problem. On three degraded 1888 pages, Amazon Textract files only 1 of 1494 figures correctly; TableVision correctly files 99.2\%, and all 51 columns its exact-total check releases are correct. A dynamic program cuts a volume into blocks by how many columns sum to their last line; witnesses read the cells; the gate classifies each column as exactly reconciling, flagged as faithful to a page whose arithmetic is wrong, or withheld. A theory of the sum check says which errors it cannot see and why its optimal block is about $θ^{-1/2}$ rows at defect rate $θ$. On 2201 columns of Wisconsin electoral data, the gate accepts 2042, flags 118, and withholds 41; failure rises with column length $R$ as $1-(1-θ)^R$ at 1.1 defects per 1000 cells in general elections; official returns confirm 933 of 934 testable accepted sums and all 35 testable flagged totals; and the deployment added 491\,359 gated precinct rows to a 9.3-million-row dataset. One implication is that the design of the verification block is critical.

</details>


### [253] [Efficient Fréchet Distributional Decoder Alignment for Latent Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17466)
*Xianghong Fang,Jiajun Zhu,Esma Aimeur,Dehan Kong,Tim G. J. Rudner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出FDDA方法，通过冻结潜在生成器、直接用FD目标在生成时潜变量上优化解码器，解决解码器训练-生成不匹配问题，显著降低gFID/gFDr⁶且大幅减少耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向潜在生成模型的FD后训练方法只优化潜在生成器而冻结解码器；解码器在真实图像的重构潜变量上训练，却在生成器产生的潜变量上部署，二者分布不一致造成“解码器训练-生成不匹配”，解码器实际面对的输入分布并非其直接训练过的分布。

Method: 提出Fréchet分布解码器对齐（FDDA）：一种高效后训练方法，冻结潜在生成器，直接以FD目标在生成时潜变量上优化解码器，仅需一个epoch。

Result: 在11个流式与自回归潜在生成模型上，单epoch FDDA持续改善gFID和gFDr⁶；相比原始预训练模型，gFDr⁶降低34.0–59.6%；在生成器侧FD后训练基础上再降低10.2–57.2%；相比生成器侧FD后训练，总墙钟时间减少3.6×–328.7×。

Conclusion: 仅做生成器侧后训练并未穷尽FD优化的收益，通过在解码器上以显著更低成本进行适配可获得进一步增益。

Abstract: Fréchet Distributional (FD) objectives provide an effective distribution-level signal for post-training generative models. For latent generative models, however, existing FD-based post-training methods optimize only the latent generator while keeping the decoder frozen: the decoder is trained on reconstruction latents obtained from real images but deployed on generator-produced latents at generation time. When these latent distributions differ, this \textbf{decoder train-generation mismatch} means that the decoder is deployed on inputs drawn from a distribution it was not directly trained on. We introduce \textbf{Fréchet Distributional Decoder Alignment (FDDA)}, an efficient post-training method that freezes the latent generator and directly optimizes the decoder on generation-time latents using an FD objective. A single epoch of FDDA consistently improves gFID and gFDr$^6$ across diverse flow-based and autoregressive latent generative models. Across all 11 evaluated models, FDDA reduces gFDr$^6$ by 34.0-59.6\% from the original pretrained models and provides a further 10.2-57.2\% reduction after generator-side FD post-training. FDDA also reduces total wall-clock time by factors of $3.6\times$-$328.7\times$ relative to generator-side FD post-training across these models. These results show that generator-side post-training alone does not exhaust the benefits of FD optimization, and that further gains can be obtained by adapting the decoder at substantially lower cost.

</details>


### [254] [Gaussian Splatting for Real-Time Scene Reconstruction: Methods, Systems, and Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17513)
*Ashim Dahal,Rabab Abdelfattah,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的综述，梳理其在稀疏视角生成、动态重建、效率与压缩、流式传输及SLAM等方面的进展，并将其归类为五个相互重叠的类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS自2023年提出后，其显式且高度可配置的场景表示被扩展到多个方向。然而，渲染已有表示与构建、更新、维护场景本身是不同的任务，现有文献缺乏系统梳理与端到端流程的连接。

Method: 综述并综合现有文献，将GS场景重建分为五个相互重叠的类别：单次生成、动态表示、效率与压缩、流式与沉浸式系统、GS SLAM。区分仅渲染吞吐量与任务相关的场景生成、更新、跟踪、建图及优化开销，并结合受控稀疏视角基准测试与原生评估。

Result: 识别出跨类别在质量、延迟、内存与更新成本方面的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 通过分类与评估对照，建立了当前方法与工具通向端到端流程的联系，并揭示了不同类别间的成本与质量权衡。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) allows for high-quality real-time novel-view synthesis. Since its inception in 2023, its explicit and highly configurable scene representation has been extended toward sparse-view generation, dynamic reconstruction, efficiency and compression, streaming, and simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). Although Gaussian Splatting can provide real-time rendering, rendering an existing representation is distinct from constructing, updating, or maintaining the scene itself. In this survey, we synthesize current literature, categorize them and provide a connection of current approaches and tools towards an end-to-end pipeline. Explicitly, we categorize GS scene reconstruction into five overlapping families: single-shot generation, dynamic representations, efficiency and compression, streaming and immersive systems, and Gaussian Splatting SLAM. We distinguish render-only throughput from task-specific scene generation, update, tracking, mapping, and optimization costs, and combine controlled sparse-view benchmarking with native evaluations to identify cross-family quality, latency, memory, and update-cost trade-offs.

</details>


### [255] [TwinPrune: Density-Aware Two-Phase Token Pruning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17530)
*Kai Liu,Anqi Li,Junxian Li,Zhixin Wang,Zhikai Chen,Renjing Pei,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的"空间-密度"信号为推理两阶段（预填充和解码）打分token重要性：预填充用二分合并（bipartite merge），解码用KV淘汰（KV eviction），在多个模型和基准上取得计算、内存、精度的三方帕累托前沿，并揭示了解码剪枝评估中的关键陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型推理压缩方法常将预填充与解码两阶段混为一谈，导致评估结论失真。论文指出一个关键评估陷阱：仅用预填充探针（如MMBench、POPE、ScienceQA，只评估首个解码token）来验证解码剪枝效果，实际测量的是预填充质量而非解码性能。因此需要统一打分信号，并对两阶段分别隔离评估。

Method: 提出统一的"空间-密度"信号衡量token重要性，同时服务两个推理阶段：预填充阶段采用二分合并（bipartite merge），解码阶段采用基于密度的top-k KV淘汰。为隔离两阶段，按打分逻辑拆分基准：预填充探针（MMBench、POPE、ScienceQA）仅评估首个解码token；全流程探针（TextVQA、DocVQA、MM-Vet）评估完整生成序列。在InternVL-3.5、Qwen3-VL、LLaVA-1.5共七个模型变体上进行验证。

Result: 在七个模型变体和三个全流程基准上取得计算、内存、精度的三方帕累托前沿。基于密度的预填充压缩保持强精度，随视觉token数线性扩展，高分辨率下token选择快100倍。解码方面，密度top-k KV淘汰在TextVQA和MM-Vet上一致优于H2O、SnapKV和StreamingLLM；TextVQA在0.125保留率下超H2O 9.6分，InternVL-3.5（2B至38B）保持7至10分优势。压缩随模型变大表现更优：8B到38B每个过渡都降低精度损失。实验还证明，解码时丢弃全部视觉KV几乎不影响预填充探针精度，却大幅降低TextVQA和MM-Vet表现，说明这种不敏感性源于打分规则而非压缩强度。

Conclusion: 解码剪枝的效果不能仅靠预填充探针验证，否则测量的是预填充质量而非解码性能。建议将MM-Vet或DocVQA作为任何解码阶段声明的强制全流程验证基准。方法在计算、内存、精度上达成三方帕累托最优，且压缩收益随模型规模增大而提升。

Abstract: We propose a unified spatial-density signal to score token importance for both inference phases: bipartite merge for prefill and KV eviction for decode, yielding measurable gains in each stage. To isolate the two phases, we split benchmarks by scoring logic. Prefill probes (MMBench, POPE, ScienceQA) evaluate only the first decoded token; their accuracy reflects prefill logits argmax before any decode KV eviction. Full-pipeline probes (TextVQA, DocVQA, MM-Vet) assess the complete generated sequence and capture both phases. We identify a critical evaluation pitfall: decode pruning claims validated solely on prefill probes measure prefill quality, not decode performance. Experiments show discarding all visual KV during decode barely affects prefill-probe accuracy but degrades TextVQA and MM-Vet drastically; this insensitivity stems from the scoring rule, not compression intensity.Evaluated on seven model variants (InternVL-3.5, Qwen3-VL, LLaVA-1.5) and three full-pipeline benchmarks, our method achieves a three-way Pareto frontier across compute, memory and accuracy. Density-based prefill compression maintains strong accuracy, scales linearly with visual token count, and delivers 100x faster token selection at high resolution. For decode, density top-k KV eviction consistently outperforms strong baselines H2O, SnapKV and StreamingLLM on TextVQA and MM-Vet across three retention ratios. At 0.125 keep ratio on TextVQA, it surpasses H2O by 9.6 points; InternVL-3.5 models (2B to 38B) maintain a 7 to 10 point advantage. Compression scales favorably with model size: every measured 8B to 38B model transition reduces accuracy loss instead of worsening it. We suggest MM-Vet or DocVQA as mandatory full-pipeline validation for any decode-phase claims. Our code is available at https://github.com/Kai-Liu001/TwinPrune

</details>


### [256] [CrossPortrait: Cross-Species Portrait Animation via Adversarial Motion Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17532)
*Gezhong Pan,Zhe Li,Yunfan Tao,Yongheng Zhao,Shiqi Zhou,Juyong Zhang,Xiaosong Jia,Yanyong Zhang,Shuquan Ye,Shaofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CrossPortrait框架，实现跨物种肖像动画，支持人→动物、动物→人及动物→动物场景，通过物种路由表情编码器与表情级对抗域适应抑制驱动物种泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨物种肖像动画因不同物种面部结构存在显著解剖学差异而极具挑战；现有方法在动物视频上微调人类肖像动画模型，常出现驱动物种泄漏问题。

Method: 将肖像运动分解为刚性头部姿态与表情关键点；设计物种路由表情编码器，在统一表情编码器中引入物种特异的LoRA以适配各物种面部结构并促进跨物种表情对齐；提出表情级对抗域适应（Ex-ADA），抑制表情潜变量中的驱动物种特有信息，缓解物种泄漏并构建跨物种共享表情空间。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法能在多种物种间实现富有表现力且保持身份一致性的肖像动画，性能优于其他最先进方法。

Conclusion: CrossPortrait有效解决了跨物种肖像动画中的驱动物种泄漏问题，实现了多场景、跨物种的高质量表情驱动动画。

Abstract: Cross-species portrait animation, which aims to animate a source portrait of species A using a driving video of species B, is particularly challenging due to substantial anatomical differences in facial structures across species. Previous works typically fine-tune human portrait animation models on animal video datasets but often suffer from driving-species leakage. To address this, we propose CrossPortrait, a novel framework for expressive cross-species portrait animation, supporting diverse scenarios including human-to-animal, animal-to-human and cross-species animal-to-animal (e.g. cat-to-dog) animation. Specifically, we decompose the portrait motion into rigid head poses and expression keypoints. To accommodate species-specific facial structures while facilitating cross-species expression alignment, we introduce a species-routed expression encoder that incorporates species-specific LoRAs into a unified expression encoder. Furthermore, we introduce expression-level adversarial domain adaptation (Ex-ADA) to suppress driving-species-specific information in the expression latents, thereby mitigating species leakage and promoting a shared expression space across species. Extensive experiments show that our method enables expressive and identity-preserving portrait animation across diverse species and outperforms other state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [257] [FlashVSR+: Efficient Video Super-Resolution via Hybrid Attention and Reward-Yield Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17552)
*Ruchang Yao,Shi Guo,Junhao Zhuang,Mingde Yao,Xin Cai,Jiahao Zhan,Bin Fu,Xinyuan Chen,Yu Qiao,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashVSR+ 通过混合注意力架构与奖励收益分布匹配蒸馏（Ry-DMD）训练策略，同时提升视频超分的模型效率与人类偏好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频超分（VSR）模型虽通过扩散蒸馏实现高效单步生成，但高分辨率推理成本仍高，且蒸馏模型难以对齐人类偏好，因此需要同时改进模型架构与训练策略。

Method: 架构上，基于预训练 VSR 模型的强局部性引入混合注意力，保留稀疏全局交互以维持长程一致性；训练上，提出奖励收益分布匹配蒸馏（Ry-DMD），利用奖励加权的教师轨迹将黑盒奖励纳入分布匹配，且无需通过奖励模型反向传播。

Result: 架构实现最高 2 倍 DiT 加速、峰值内存降低 45%，可在单张 24GB RTX 4090 上进行空间不分块的 4K VSR；Ry-DMD 可泛化至 T2I 生成，并在用户偏好上取得一致提升。

Conclusion: FlashVSR+ 在恢复质量、高分辨率效率及用户偏好对齐方面均有显著提升，代码、模型与训练数据已开源。

Abstract: Recent video super-resolution (VSR) models achieve efficient one-step generation through diffusion distillation. However, high-resolution inference remains costly, and distilled models are difficult to align with human preferences. These challenges call for improvements in both model architecture and training strategy. We present \textbf{FlashVSR+} to address both limitations. On the architecture side, we introduce hybrid attention based on the strong locality observed in pretrained VSR models, while retaining sparse global interaction for long-range consistency. It achieves up to $2\times$ DiT speedup and 45\% lower peak memory, enabling spatially untiled 4K VSR on a single 24GB RTX 4090. On the training side, we propose Reward-Yield Distribution Matching Distillation (Ry-DMD) for preference-aligned one-step generation. Ry-DMD uses reward-weighted teacher rollouts to incorporate black-box rewards into distribution matching, without backpropagating through the reward model. We further show that Ry-DMD generalizes beyond VSR to T2I generation. Together, FlashVSR+ delivers stronger restoration quality and substantially improves high-resolution efficiency, with consistent gains in user preference. We release our code, models, and training data at \href{https://www.flashvsr.tech}{\textcolor{FlashBlue}{\textbf{www.flashvsr.tech}}}.

</details>


### [258] [Benchmarking Self-Supervised 3D Medical Vision Foundation Models and Transferability Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17573)
*Jiaqi Tang,Yu Wang,Yang Liu,Jing Tao,Fandong Zhang,Zhou Zhen,Xiahai Zhuang,Qingchao Chen,Yining Yang,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对23个3D医学基础模型在14个任务上进行统一基准评测，并提出InsightRank在微调前对候选模型排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发表的3D医学自监督预训练模型评测使用不同数据集、指标和微调协议，模型在架构、预训练目标和数据上也各不相同，导致难以直接比较，也无法为具体新任务选型提供可比的依据。直接微调比较计算成本过高。

Method: 在相同的14个分割、分类和回归任务（覆盖多种解剖部位与模态）上基准测试23个候选模型；并将多种可迁移性估计器与微调后的真实排名进行对比。提出InsightRank，利用任务适配的冻结特征统计和架构感知的成对比较，在微调前对候选模型排序。

Result: 没有任何模型能在所有分割任务或所有分类回归任务中均进入前三；同一预训练目标下卷积编码器与Transformer在不同任务上互有胜负。InsightRank在23个模型池上的平均Kendall tau-b为分割0.431、分类回归0.352；分割Top 3在九个任务中有四个包含任务最优模型，而图像级Top 3在五个任务中均未包含。三模型候选短名单在七个分割任务上将抽取加训练总耗时减少85.8%。

Conclusion: 该基准为3D医学基础模型选择提供了共同参考，InsightRank提供了一种低成本优先微调候选的方法，但其短名单效果因目标任务类型而异。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining has expanded the pool of 3D medical foundation models, but published evaluations use different datasets, endpoints and fine-tuning protocols. Models also differ in architecture, pretraining objective and data, while downstream targets range from voxel-level masks to patient-level diagnoses and outcomes. As a result, published findings provide little directly comparable evidence for choosing a model for a new task. Direct comparison of adaptation performance requires fine-tuning candidates on labelled target data, at substantial computational cost. We benchmark 23 candidates across the same 14 segmentation, classification and regression tasks spanning multiple anatomies and modalities. No candidate ranked among the top three across all segmentation tasks or across all classification and regression tasks; a convolutional encoder could outperform a transformer trained with the same objective on one task but underperform it on another. We benchmark transferability estimators against the fine-tuned rankings and find that their agreement varies across dense segmentation, classification and regression. InsightRank uses task-appropriate frozen-feature statistics and architecture-aware pairwise comparisons to rank candidates before fine-tuning. Across the 23-model pool, mean Kendall tau-b was 0.431 for segmentation and 0.352 for classification and regression. The segmentation Top 3 contained a task winner on four of nine tasks; the image-level Top 3 contained none on the five tasks, despite better full-ranking agreement than the matched conventional estimators. A three-model shortlist reduced recorded extraction-plus-training job time by 85.8% across seven segmentation tasks. Together, the benchmark provides a common reference for model selection and InsightRank offers a lower-cost way to prioritize fine-tuning, with shortlist performance that differs by target type.

</details>


### [259] [Physical-Field-Bridged Material Parameter Inference from Multi-View Deformation Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17591)
*Linrui Shou,Jie Li,Yu Liu,Tao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出以稠密物理场为桥梁的单次前向材质推断框架，从多视角RGB形变视频同时实现本构类型分类与连续参数回归，并构建了DeformFieldBench基准，在速度与精度上显著优于逐场景优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真可用材质参数对可形变物体操作至关重要，但材料属性只能通过物理响应间接体现，估计困难；既有逆物理方法依赖逐场景重建、在线调参或仿真在环优化，速度慢且易受外观与类别捷径影响。

Method: 提出"物理场桥接"的单次推断框架：先从多视角RGB形变视频预测力/接触掩码、投影运动流场与投影应力响应图，再以这些稠密物理场为条件进行本构类型分类和连续参数回归，从而降低对表观与类别的依赖。同时构建DeformFieldBench：基于3DGS与MPM的仿真学习环境，含100个物体形状、5种力学信息载荷模式共5000次仿真，并提供像素对齐的稠密物理监督。

Result: 在全部四个连续参数上取得最优中位误差；应力热点召回率由0.0762提升至0.5121；未见物体同仿真器回放达0.8676 SSIM与36.82 dB PSNR。在协议匹配的GSO评测中，推断与未来状态预测约需30秒，而逐场景优化需15–120分钟；在5段真实序列上也有可测量的回放一致性。

Conclusion: 稠密物理场可作为快速材质推断的有效桥梁；但跨仿真器与真实世界测试仍暴露出域差距与可辨识性方面的不足，有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Estimating simulation-ready material parameters from visual observations is crucial for deformable-object manipulation but challenging because material properties are only indirectly revealed through physical responses. We introduce physical-field-bridged material inference, a single-pass framework that uses dense physical responses to connect multi-view RGB deformation videos with constitutive-type classification and continuous parameter regression. Unlike inverse-physics methods requiring per-scene reconstruction, online tuning, or simulation-in-the-loop optimization, our model predicts force/contact masks, projected motion-flow fields, and projected stress-response maps, then conditions material inference on these fields to reduce reliance on appearance or category shortcuts. We construct DeformFieldBench, a 3DGS MPM learning environment comprising 5,000 simulations across 100 object shapes and five mechanically informative loading modes, with dense pixel-aligned physical supervision. Our method achieves the best median errors on all four continuous parameters, improves stress-hotspot recall from 0.0762 to 0.5121, and attains 0.8676 SSIM/36.82 dB PSNR in same-simulator replay on unseen objects. Under a protocol-matched GSO evaluation, inference and future-state prediction take approximately 30 seconds, compared with 15-120 minutes for per-scene optimization. The method also yields measurable replay agreement on five real-world sequences. These results support dense physical fields as an effective bridge for fast material inference, while cross-simulator and real-world tests reveal remaining domain and identifiability gaps.

</details>


### [260] [Tanawpamana: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Philippine Cultural Heritage Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17596)
*Ivan Yuri De Leon,Hiroki Ouchi,Sakriani Sakti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Tanawpamana数据集与基准，用于评测视觉语言模型对菲律宾文化遗产地的识别与知识表现，发现模型在细粒度视觉区分和图像推断地理知识方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在识别与描述地点方面应用渐广，但对其特定文化（尤其菲律宾等代表性不足地区）的识别能力与知识储备了解甚少。

Method: 构建Tanawpamana数据集，包含菲律宾文化遗产地图像及基于国家文化艺术委员会Talapamana名录的元数据；并设计Tanawpamana基准，含4,046项任务，用于评测模型对遗产地的识别与知识；对七个视觉语言模型进行评估。

Result: 模型在相似遗产地之间的细粒度视觉区分上表现弱于粗粒度识别；且从图像输入推断地理知识的表现弱于从文本推断。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型对菲律宾文化遗产地的识别与知识仍存在明显短板，尤其在细粒度视觉判别和图像驱动的地理知识推断方面有待提升。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used to identify and describe places, yet their recognition and knowledge of specific cultures remain poorly understood, particularly in underrepresented regions such as the Philippines. This study introduces the Tanawpamana Dataset, a repository of Philippine heritage site images with metadata based on the Talapamana registry of the National Commission for Culture and the Arts of the Philippines. We also introduce the Tanawpamana Benchmark, comprising 4,046 tasks designed to evaluate models' recognition and knowledge of heritage sites. Evaluating seven vision-language models, we find that they struggle more with fine-grained visual discrimination among similar heritage sites than with coarse recognition, and that their geographic knowledge is weaker when inferred from image inputs than from text.

</details>


### [261] [Morphology-aware deep learning predicts colorectal cancer survival from whole-slide images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17603)
*Hikmat Khan,Metin N. Gurcan,Anil Parwani,Wendy L. Frankel,Wei Chen,Muhammad Khalid Khan Niazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MorphContrast，一种形态感知的对比多实例学习框架，利用组织形态标签作为结构监督，从H&E全切片图像预测结直肠癌五年生存，在多个队列中取得AUC 0.64-0.70的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从H&E全切片图像预测五年生存有助于风险分层，但该任务属于弱监督，且携带预后信息的组织形态尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 回顾性二次分析：TCGA-COAD/READ（n=562）做形态特异性分析，Alliance/CALGB 89803（n=424）建模，SurGen（n=218）外部评估。MorphContrast采用门控注意力池化，并结合三个辅助目标：patch级形态标签定义患者内对比，结局一致/不一致在匹配形态内定义患者间对比，多样性正则化防止表征坍缩；结局不一致患者的形态匹配patch作为难负样本。

Result: 形态特异性模型判别力弱但分布广泛：脂肪组织AUC 0.622，间质0.604，与肿瘤上皮（0.601-0.605）相当。Alliance/CALGB 89803上单模型AUC 0.642，事后集成pooled out-of-fold AUC达0.70，风险组HR 3.79（95%CI 2.55-5.63）。外部SurGen结肠癌队列（n=218）单模型与集成AUC分别为0.669和0.678。

Conclusion: 结果支持将组织形态作为结构监督，用于结直肠癌预后中结局感知的表征学习。

Abstract: Background: Five-year survival prediction from hematoxylin-and-eosin whole-slide images could support risk stratification, but the task is weakly supervised, and the tissue morphologies carrying prognostic information remain incompletely characterized.
  Methods: We conducted a retrospective secondary analysis using TCGA-COAD/READ (n=562) for morphology-specific analysis, Alliance/ CALGB 89803 (n=424) for model development, and SurGen (n=218) for external evaluation. We propose MorphContrast, a morphology-aware contrastive multiple-instance learning framework that uses tissue labels as structural supervision for five-year survival prediction. MorphContrast combines gated-attention pooling with three auxiliary objectives: patch-level morphology labels define within-patient contrasts, outcome concordance or discordance defines cross-patient contrasts within matched morphologies, and diversity regularization discourages representational collapse. Morphology-matched patches from patients with discordant outcomes serve as hard negatives, encouraging prognostic discrimination within comparable histologic contexts.
  Results: Morphology-specific models show weak but distributed prognostic discrimination: adipose tissue achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.622, stroma achieves 0.604, and both perform comparably to tumor epithelium (0.601-0.605). On Alliance/CALGB 89803, the single model achieves an AUC of 0.642, while the post hoc ensemble reaches a pooled out-of-fold AUC of 0.70 and a risk-group hazard ratio of 3.79 (95% confidence interval: 2.55-5.63). On the external SurGen colon cancer cohort (n=218), the single model and ensemble achieve AUCs of 0.669 and 0.678, respectively.
  Conclusions: These findings support tissue morphology as structural supervision for outcome-aware representation learning in colorectal cancer prognosis.

</details>


### [262] [PLEDGE: Progressive Lossy Error-Bounded Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17605)
*Mingze Xia,Yuxiao Li,Baixi Sun,Wenbo Li,Pu Jiao,Sheng Di,Franck Cappello,Hanqi Guo,Xin Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PLEDGE 是一种免训练、渐进式且带误差上界的 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）编解码器，可将四种用户指定的容差转化为逐图元的量化边界，并在整个相机域内（含未见视角）保证误差，同时组装成带显式误差界的前缀渐进码流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 编解码器仅报告压缩后的重建质量，用户难以控制单个图元的失真。作者希望让用户按应用需求，对位置、足迹变形、视角相关颜色和透明度等机制的失真分别设定容差，从而有针对性地分配码率。

Method: PLEDGE 将四种用户声明的容差（屏幕空间位置、旋转与缩放引起的足迹变形、视角相关颜色、透明度）转化为逐图元的量化界：位置和透明度直接设界；足迹变形在光栅化器的仿射投影模型下设界；颜色在所有观察方向上设界。这些界在声明的相机域内（包括未见视角）成立。量化的残差被组织为渐进式码流，并为每个有效前缀提供显式误差界。该方法无需训练。

Result: 在十三个场景上的实验验证了所有声明的逐机制误差界，并展现出有竞争力的率失真性能。四种容差带来的码率开销差异显著，用户可将码率花在应用更需要的机制上。

Conclusion: PLEDGE 提供了一个免训练、渐进、带误差上界的 3DGS 编解码框架，使用户能对逐图元的失真进行细粒度控制，并在未见视角下保证误差界，同时保持有竞争力的率失真表现。

Abstract: Existing 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) codecs report reconstruction quality after compression, and give users little control over the distortion of individual primitives. We present PLEDGE, a training-free, progressive, error-bounded codec that translates four user-declared tolerances into per-primitive quantization bounds: screen-space position, footprint deformation due to rotation and scale, view-dependent color, and opacity. The bounds apply throughout a declared camera domain, including previously unseen viewpoints. Position and opacity are bounded directly; footprint deformation is bounded under the rasterizer's affine projection model, and color is bounded over all viewing directions. PLEDGE organizes quantized residuals into a progressive bitstream with explicit error bounds for every valid prefix. Experiments across thirteen scenes verify all declared per-mechanism bounds and demonstrate competitive rate-distortion performance. The four tolerances incur substantially different rate costs, so users can spend rate where their application needs it.

</details>


### [263] [Smartphone-Based, Field-Deployed AI Inspection System for Power Distribution Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17620)
*Sampath Balaji Masabathula,Srija Vanapalli,Gangadhar Kanchi,Sreenivasa Rao V. A. N,Vijaya Lalitha,Sasank Chilamkurthy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并试点部署了一套基于智能手机的AI配电设施巡检系统，在多级工作流中融合图像质量校验、深度学习模型、单目深度估计与几何/规则分析，用于检测电杆倾斜、横担与顶夹倾斜、电压等级、植被净空风险及配变对地距离等缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网络地域分散，传统人工巡检难以一致、规模化地开展；需要一种可由现场人员便捷使用、可系统化识别与跟踪缺陷的巡检方案。

Method: 构建智能手机端AI巡检系统：先进行图像质量与拍摄视角校验，再结合深度学习模型、单目深度估计、几何分析与规则处理，形成统一的多级巡检工作流，覆盖电杆倾斜、横担/顶夹倾斜、电压等级、植被净空风险和配变（DTR）对地净空等评估项；在APEPDCL五个配电馈线上试点部署，并建立整改与复核跟踪流程；同时公开PD-Defect现场采集多模块数据集。

Result: 试点中识别缺陷1,222例，研究期内记录整改完成84例；馈线级停电记录也被分析，但现有观测不足以证明所提系统带来可靠性提升。

Conclusion: 现场部署表明，智能手机AI辅助巡检可实际融入巡检与运维流程，为配电设施目标缺陷的系统化识别、记录、整改跟踪与复核提供机制；系统可靠性收益尚需更多证据支撑。

Abstract: Routine inspection of power distribution infrastructure remains challenging for utilities operating geographically dispersed networks, where conventional manual inspection can be difficult to perform consistently and at scale. This work presents a smartphone-based AI inspection system for assessing targeted distribution-infrastructure conditions from images captured by field personnel. The proposed workflow integrates image-quality and viewing-perspective validation with AI-assisted assessment of pole lean, crossarm and top-cleat tilt, voltage level, vegetation-clearance risk, and distribution-transformer (DTR)-ground clearance. The system integrates deep-learning models with monocular depth estimation, geometric analysis, and rule-based processing within a common multi-stage inspection workflow designed for practical field operation. The system was pilot deployed across five selected distribution feeders within the Eastern Power Distribution Company of Andhra Pradesh Ltd. (APEPDCL), where 1,222 defect instances were identified across the assessed categories and subsequently tracked through a rectification and verification workflow, with 84 defect instances recorded as rectified during the study period. Feeder-level interruption records were also examined over the study period; however, the available observations do not provide sufficient evidence to establish a reliability improvement attributable to the proposed system. A curated multi-module field-captured dataset, PD-Defect, is also publicly released to support reproducible research in distribution-infrastructure inspection. The field deployment demonstrates the practical integration of smartphone-based AI-assisted inspection into field inspection and maintenance activities, providing a mechanism for systematic identification, documentation, rectification tracking, and verification of targeted distribution-infrastructure defects.

</details>


### [264] [4LC: 4D Latent Cache for Efficient Conditioning in Dynamic World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17625)
*Jianhui Zhang,Zuyao Lin,Pengxin Xu,Jingdong Wang,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出4LC——一种带复合视觉表征与灵活时空访问接口的4D潜缓存，用于视频生成中的动态世界建模，并基于此构建4LC-Video模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态世界建模的视频生成中，观测到的静态与动态内容应在不同时间和视角的重复生成中被高效复用作为条件。但传统方式需逐请求进行RGB渲染与重新编码，效率低，且单一视觉编码器的表征信息有限，难以统一支持多种下游任务。

Method: 1）4LC作为潜缓存，直接复用静态与动态内容的视觉表征作为生成条件，避免逐请求的RGB渲染与再编码；2）作为复合缓存，将多种视觉编码器（如VAE、DINO、V-JEPA、VGGT）的表征绑定到共享的几何与运动上，形成信息丰富的复合表征；3）设计灵活的时空接口，可在独立指定的时间与视角上直接读取缓存，并跨观测组合读数，为下游任务提供统一的条条件格式；4）基于4LC构建4LC-Video，通过配置源观测、时空请求与历史复用方式，支持相机控制、时间控制、新视角合成、场景重访和长期生成等任务。

Result: 实验表明，单一4LC-Video模型无需针对具体任务微调，即可在多个任务上实现更高效的条件获取、有效的时空可控性以及良好的场景一致性。

Conclusion: 4LC通过潜缓存复用与复合表征、灵活时空接口，为动态世界视频生成提供了统一高效的条件范式，使单一模型无需任务特定微调即可覆盖多种时空控制与生成任务。

Abstract: For video generation in dynamic world modeling, observed static and dynamic content should be efficiently reused to provide conditions for repeated generation at different times and viewpoints. We introduce 4LC, a 4D latent cache with composite visual representations and a flexible spatiotemporal access interface. As a latent cache, 4LC directly reuses visual representations of static and dynamic content to provide generation conditions, avoiding per-request RGB rendering and re-encoding. As a composite cache, 4LC binds visual representations from multiple visual encoders (e.g., VAE, DINO, V-JEPA, VGGT) to shared geometry and motion, allowing the composite representations to describe the dynamic world with rich information. Besides, we design a flexible spatiotemporal interface, which directly reads the cache at independently specified times and viewpoints and combines readouts across observations, providing a common conditioning format for diverse downstream tasks. Building on 4LC, we develop 4LC-Video to address multiple video generation tasks through different configurations of source observations, spatiotemporal requests, and historical reuse, including camera control, temporal control, novel-view synthesis, scene revisits, and long-term generation. Our experiments show more efficient condition acquisition, effective spatiotemporal controllability, and scene consistency across tasks using a single 4LC-Video model without task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [265] [FrameLite: Decision-Oriented Aesthetic Cropping with a Compact Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17639)
*Jianze Li,Kai Liu,Ziqing Zhang,Weihang Zhang,Yong Guo,Linghe Kong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrameLite 是一个基于 Qwen3.5-0.8B 的轻量级美学图像裁剪模型，通过三阶段训练让其直接输出高质量裁剪坐标，在公开基准上超越更大的 VLM 裁剪模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型虽能引入语义知识，但其规模限制了轻量级摄影辅助应用；同时观察发现小模型生成的最优裁剪往往优于其首次给出的答案，存在未被利用的潜力。

Method: 三阶段训练：第一阶段的空间与构图训练建立定位和取景偏好；第二阶段质量引导在线监督（QOS）从当前策略生成的候选中学习最高质量的完整答案；第三阶段贪婪参照优化（GRO）利用相对当前贪婪答案的正面与负面质量差异进一步优化策略。两个在线阶段均使用标注派生反馈，推理时直接输出一个坐标答案，无需搜索或外部评分器。

Result: 在公开基准上，FrameLite 以明显更小的骨干网络超越了所比较的大型 VLM 裁剪模型；控制实验证实，选取高质量训练答案比仅靠在线采样更能提升最终模型。

Conclusion: 小模型具备超过其首次预测的裁剪潜力，通过高质量答案筛选与在线优化可充分释放，从而实现轻量且高效的美学图像裁剪，代码与模型权重将开源。

Abstract: Aesthetic image cropping improves composition by balancing subjects, context, and image boundaries. Large vision-language models bring semantic knowledge to this task, but their scale limits lightweight photography assistance. We propose FrameLite, a compact cropping model built on Qwen3.5-0.8B. Our approach is motivated by the observation that a small model can generate better crops than its first answer suggests. We develop three-stage training to turn this potential into stronger direct predictions. Spatial and composition training establishes localization and framing preferences. Quality-Guided Online Supervision (QOS) then learns the highest-quality complete answers among the current policy's generated candidates. Greedy-Referenced Optimization (GRO) further refines the policy using positive and negative quality differences relative to its current greedy answer. Both online stages use annotation-derived feedback, while inference returns one coordinate answer without search or an external scorer. Experiments show that FrameLite outperforms the compared large-VLM croppers across public benchmarks with a substantially smaller backbone. Controlled studies confirm that selecting high-quality training answers improves the final model beyond online sampling alone. We will release our code and model weights at https://github.com/JianzeLi-114/FrameLite .

</details>


### [266] [MemAct: Streaming Spatial Understanding with Memory and Active Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17640)
*Yuxi Wei,Wei Huang,Qirui Chen,Lu Hou,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MemAct 框架，将记忆写入/检索与主动探索统一在流式空间理解循环中，并构建约 1M 训练样本及含 20,000 主 QA 对的 MemAct-bench，实验表明其优于现有流式基线并接近前沿多模态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体在增量观测下需流式推理，但任务相关证据往往既需回忆历史观测，也需主动获取新视角；现有流式方法侧重处理已到达观测，缺乏任务驱动的主动探索。

Method: 共享视觉-语言模型增量写入结构化历史记忆，按需检索记录与过去帧，并通过移动与转向获取额外证据；检索证据与动作观测共同影响后续决策，形成统一推理循环。训练上先用监督微调学习记忆与交互，再用累积证据增益、动作成本与终止答案质量进行反事实动作优化。

Result: 构建 MemAct 套件（数据生成流程与真实/仿真环境采集，约 1M 训练样本，覆盖空间推理、记忆写入、检索与探索）；MemAct-bench 含 20,000 主 QA 对（当前观测理解、历史证据使用、主动探索）及 2,000 条独立 Timing 问题。实验显示 MemAct 优于现有空间理解与流式基线，在 MemAct-bench 上接近前沿多模态模型，同时保持流式推理效率。

Conclusion: 将记忆与行动纳入统一流式推理循环，可有效提升具身智能体的空间理解与主动探索及停止决策能力，并为该方向提供数据、基准与训练方法。

Abstract: Spatial understanding is essential for embodied agents navigating and interacting with the physical world. As observations arrive incrementally, agents must reason in a streaming manner. Yet streaming understanding must also be closed-loop: task-relevant evidence may require both recalling past observations and actively acquiring new views. Existing streaming approaches largely emphasize processing incoming observations, leaving task-driven active exploration underexplored. We introduce MemAct, a framework for streaming spatial understanding with memory and action. A shared vision--language model incrementally writes structured historical memory, retrieves records and past frames on demand, and acquires additional evidence through movement and turning. Retrieved evidence and action observations inform subsequent decisions, enabling the agent to continue exploring or answer within a unified reasoning loop. We develop the MemAct suite, a data generation pipeline and collection across real and simulated environments, with approximately 1M training examples for spatial reasoning, memory writing, retrieval, and exploration. Its MemAct-bench comprises 20,000 main QA pairs evaluating current-observation understanding, historical evidence use, and active exploration, plus 2,000 separate Timing questions. MemAct combines supervised fine-tuning for memory and interaction with counterfactual action optimization using cumulative evidence gains, action costs, and terminal answer quality to improve exploration and stopping decisions. Experiments show that MemAct outperforms existing spatial understanding and streaming baselines and approaches frontier multimodal models on MemAct-bench while retaining streaming inference efficiency.

</details>


### [267] [Open-o3-Video-v2: Seek, Ground, and Reason with Verifiable Spatio-Temporal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17656)
*Jiahao Meng,Zhongyuan Bao,Yue Tan,Haiquan Wen,Renrui Zhang,Anna Wang,Jason Li,Yanwei Fu,Yunhai Tong,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Open-o3-Video-v2，将视频推理形式化为统一的智能体式证据落地过程，让模型主动识别缺失信息、获取额外观测并写入时间戳与物体框证据，配合 CS-GRPO 强化学习，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的视频推理不仅需要正确答案，还需提供支撑性的视觉证据。此前 Open-o3-Video 虽能将时间戳与物体级边界框嵌入推理轨迹，但其证据仅局限于初始稀疏采样得到的帧，无法在推理过程中主动补充所需观测，限制了证据的完整性与可靠性。

Method: 作者将视频推理重构为统一的智能体式证据落地流程：模型识别缺失信息、主动获取额外观测、对支撑推理的时刻与物体进行定位，并将证据写入推理轨迹后再继续或作答。为此构建了包含 52K 多轮智能体式落地推理轨迹的 STGR-v2-CoT135K 及用于强化学习的 STGR-v2-RL-13K，并提出 Clue-Seek GRPO（CS-GRPO），通过追踪工具调用对累积证据的改变，奖励线索覆盖与逐步增益，惩罚过度、重复或无信息的检索。

Result: 在 V-STaR 上，Open-o3-Video-v2-4B 取得 40.4% mAM 和 56.3% mLGM，分别超过其 Qwen3-VL-4B 骨干 8.5% 和 12.6%，并在通用视频理解、长视频推理和时间定位等基准上均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，主动获取证据与可验证的物体级落地是相辅相成的，二者结合对实现可靠的视频推理至关重要。代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Reliable video reasoning requires models to produce correct answers and expose supporting visual evidence. Open-o3-Video enables verifiable reasoning by embedding timestamps and object-level bounding boxes into reasoning traces, but is restricted to frames from an initial sparse sampler. We introduce Open-o3-Video-v2, which formulates video reasoning as a unified agentic evidencegrounded process. The model identifies missing information, acquires additional observations, grounds the moments and objects supporting its reasoning, and writes this evidence into the trajectory before continuing or answering. To support this behavior, we construct STGR-v2-CoT135K, including 52K multi-turn agentic grounded reasoning trajectories, and STGR-v2-RL-13K for reinforcement learning. We further propose Clue-Seek GRPO (CS-GRPO), which tracks how tool calls change accumulated evidence, rewards clue coverage and step-wise gains, and penalizes excessive, repetitive, or uninformative retrieval. On V-STaR, Open-o3-Video-v2-4B achieves 40.4% mAM and 56.3% mLGM, outperforming its Qwen3-VL-4B backbone by 8.5% and 12.6%, respectively. It also delivers consistent gains across general video understanding, long-video reasoning, and temporal grounding benchmarks. These results show that active evidence acquisition and verifiable object-level grounding are complementary for reliable video reasoning. The code and models are released at https://msalab-pku.github.io/projects/Open-o3-Video-v2.

</details>


### [268] [ScenePercept: Scenario-Conditioned Perceptual Assessment of Real-World User-Generated Content Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17661)
*Shuhan Xia,Yu Chen,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Peipie Li,Zekun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ScenePercept-Bench 基准（包含 23,100 张图像、21 个类别、105 个细粒度场景，15 个属性标注）以及 SCPA 训练框架，以解决 UGC 图像感知评估中场景多样性与感知复杂性难以捕捉的问题，在 UGC 感知任务上取得 SOTA 并有效泛化到 IQA 与 IAA 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动成像与社交平台的快速发展导致 UGC 图像爆发式增长，对其进行系统全面的感知评估日益重要。但现有图像感知基准通常是任务导向的，为质量或美学评估裁剪特定场景，并在不同场景中应用统一的感知标准，限制了其捕捉真实世界 UGC 图像场景多样性与感知复杂性的能力。

Method: 提出 ScenePercept-Bench 基准，反映真实 UGC 图像分布，含 23,100 张图像、21 个类别、105 个细粒度场景。设计分层、场景感知的标注协议，覆盖视觉质量、美学感知与场景语义，共 15 个属性；每张图像标注整体分数、15 个属性分数及结构化解释性推理。此外提出 Scenario-Conditioned Perceptual Alignment（SCPA）训练框架，联合优化绝对分数预测、偏好排序与跨场景校准，在保持通用感知评估能力的同时鼓励模型探索并适应不同场景的感知标准。

Result: 大量实验表明，SCPA 在 UGC 图像感知上达到最先进性能，并能有效泛化到已有的 IQA 与 IAA 基准。

Conclusion: ScenePercept-Bench 与 SCPA 为真实场景下 UGC 图像的感知评估提供了更贴近分布的基准与场景条件化的对齐框架，兼顾通用能力与跨场景适应性。

Abstract: The rapid development of mobile imaging and social platforms has led to explosive growth in user-generated content images, making their systematic and comprehensive perceptual assessment increasingly important. Existing image perception benchmarks are typically task-oriented, curating specific scenarios for quality or aesthetic assessment and applying a uniform perceptual criterion across diverse scenarios. This limits their ability to capture the scene diversity and perceptual complexity of real-world UGC images. To address these limitations, we introduce ScenePercept-Bench, a benchmark designed to reflect the distribution of real-world UGC images, comprising 23,100 images across 21 categories and 105 fine-grained scenes. To account for the influence of scene context on human perception, we design a hierarchical, scene-aware annotation protocol, covering visual quality, aesthetic perception, and scene semantics, with 15 attributes in total. Each image is annotated with an overall score, 15 attribute-level scores, and structured explanatory reasoning. Building on this benchmark, we further introduce Scenario-Conditioned Perceptual Alignment, a training framework that tackles the heterogeneity of perceptual criteria across scenarios. SCPA jointly optimizes absolute score prediction, preference ranking, and cross-scenario calibration, preserving the model general perceptual assessment ability while encouraging it to explore and adapt perceptual criteria across different scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SCPA achieves state-of-the-art performance in UGC image perception and generalizes effectively to established IQA and IAA benchmarks.

</details>


### [269] [Hierarchical Curriculum Learning for Surgical Video Generation: Focusing on Bilateral Axillo-Breast Approach Robotic Thyroidectomy Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17684)
*Youngbin Kong,Seungho Lee,Jae Kyung Shin,Jong-hyuk Ahn,Hyoun-Joong Kong,Kyu Eun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结合分层手术标注与课程迁移学习的手术视频生成框架，在缺乏基准数据的术式上实现高质量生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术视频生成对手术教育、模拟及下游AI训练日益重要，但传统仿真管线受成本、真实感和场景多样性限制；而通用文本到视频扩散模型难以适配手术场景，原因包括复杂解剖结构、精细的工具-组织交互、手术流程的层级性，以及除少数成熟术式外缺乏整理好的训练数据。

Method: 将手术流程分解为三层（阶段、任务、动作），把预训练文本到视频扩散模型从粗到细逐步适配手术语境，并采用动态采样策略处理变分辨率和变长的手术片段。在双侧腋乳入路机器人甲状腺切除术上实例化该框架，此前该术式没有整理好的基准。

Result: 相比先前任务级模型，最终的动作级模型将FVD降低20.1%、FID降低15.7%；匹配预算的消融实验表明增益来自课程设计而非额外训练。六名临床医生的用户研究对视觉真实感评分较高，但指出在解剖准确性和临床可靠性方面仍有不足。

Conclusion: 该框架为在整理数据稀缺的术式中实现AI驱动的手术模拟迈出了一步。

Abstract: High-fidelity surgical video generation is increasingly important for surgical education, simulation, and downstream AI training, as conventional simulation pipelines are limited by cost, realism, and scenario diversity. Adapting generic text-to-video diffusion models to surgical procedures remains difficult, however, due to complex anatomical structures, intricate tool-tissue interactions, the inherently hierarchical nature of surgical workflows, and the scarcity of curated training data outside a few well-studied procedures. We propose a surgical video generation framework that combines hierarchical procedural annotation with curriculum transfer learning. Surgical procedures are decomposed into three levels (phase, task, and action) and a pretrained text-to-video diffusion model is progressively adapted from coarse to fine-grained surgical context, supported by a dynamic sampling strategy that handles variable-resolution and variable-length surgical clips. We instantiate the framework on Bilateral Axillo-Breast Approach robotic thyroidectomy, a procedure for which no curated benchmark currently exists. Relative to the preceding task-level model, the final action-level model reduces FVD by 20.1% and FID by 15.7%, and a matched-budget ablation indicates that these gains arise from the curriculum design rather than from additional training. A user study with six clinicians rates visual realism highly while highlighting remaining gaps in anatomical accuracy and clinical reliability. The proposed framework offers a step toward AI-driven surgical simulation in procedures where curated data are scarce.

</details>


### [270] [SWORD: Scene-Grounded Wasserstein Object-Relation Distance for 3D Scene Graph Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17692)
*U-Chae Jun,Jaeeun Ko,Yoonhyuk Choi,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SWORD，一种基于对齐图的 Wasserstein 距离监督方法，用于 3D 场景图预测，超越传统精确标签分类损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 场景图预测多用对象节点和关系边分类损失，只鼓励精确标签恢复，不直接优化与真实场景图的语义接近度；两个错误图可能得到相近惩罚，即使其中一个在对象角色和关系上下文上更接近。直接图距离损失因图匹配和图编辑距离需要对应搜索与编辑代价设计而较难实现。

Method: 作者观察到现有监督式 3D 场景图预测在固定对象节点索引和监督边槽上进行，从而可将场景图比较转化为定义良好的对齐图距离问题。为此提出 SWORD：通过确定性 base-to-context 顺序，从训练场景图标注构建块归一化的对象和关系 ground distance 度量，不依赖外部语义先验；再将这些度量提升为对齐节点边际与空增广边边际上的 Wasserstein 距离。

Result: SWORD 在保留真实图作为对齐边际概率空间中唯一零距离解的同时，提供超越精确标签惩罚的语义图距离监督。论文证明 SWORD 是对齐边际概率场景图上的严格度量，保持场景图 grounded 替换排序，控制加性图 grounded 语义可观测量，并给出解码语义误差的代理上界。

Conclusion: SWORD 将固定节点索引与边槽协议转化为对齐图距离监督问题，通过 Wasserstein 形式实现对 3D 场景图预测的语义距离优化，并提供度量性与误差上界等理论保证。

Abstract: 3D scene graph prediction is commonly optimized with object-node and relation-edge classification losses. These objectives encourage exact object and relation label recovery, but they do not directly optimize semantic proximity to the ground-truth scene graph: two wrong graphs can receive comparable penalties even when one is much closer in object roles and relation contexts. A direct graph-distance loss is difficult in general, since graph matching and graph edit distance require correspondence search and edit-cost design. Our key observation is that prior supervised 3D scene graph prediction protocols are conducted on fixed object-node indices and supervised edge slots.
